// Zip array - simplified version
struct ZipArray<T> {
    data: Vec<T>,
}

impl<T> ZipArray<T> {
    fn new() -> Self {
        Self { data: Vec::new() }
    }

    fn resize(&mut self, new_size: usize, preserve_data: bool) -> ZipResult<()> {
        if preserve_data {
            self.data.resize(new_size, unsafe { mem::zeroed() });
        } else {
            self.data = Vec::with_capacity(new_size);
            self.data.resize(new_size, unsafe { mem::zeroed() });
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    fn clear(&mut self) {
        self.data.clear();
    }

    fn len(&self) -> usize {
        self.data.len()
    }

    fn as_slice(&self) -> &[T] {
        &self.data
    }

    fn as_mut_slice(&mut self) -> &mut [T] {
        &mut self.data
    }

    fn get(&self, index: usize) -> Option<&T> {
        self.data.get(index)
    }

    fn get_mut(&mut self, index: usize) -> Option<&mut T> {
        self.data.get_mut(index)
    }
}

// Internal state
struct ZipInternalState {
    central_dir: ZipArray<u8>,
    central_dir_offsets: ZipArray<u32>,
    sorted_central_dir_offsets: ZipArray<u32>,
    init_flags: u32,
    zip64: bool,
    zip64_has_extended_info_fields: bool,
    file: Option<File>,
    file_archive_start_ofs: u64,
    mem_data: Vec<u8>,
    mem_size: usize,
    mem_capacity: usize,
}

impl ZipInternalState {
    fn new() -> Self {
        Self {
            central_dir: ZipArray::new(),
            central_dir_offsets: ZipArray::new(),
            sorted_central_dir_offsets: ZipArray::new(),
            init_flags: 0,
            zip64: false,
            zip64_has_extended_info_fields: false,
            file: None,
            file_archive_start_ofs: 0,
            mem_data: Vec::new(),
            mem_size: 0,
            mem_capacity: 0,
        }
    }
}

// Main zip archive structure
pub struct ZipArchive<R: Read + Seek> {
    reader: R,
    archive_size: u64,
    total_files: u32,
    central_directory_file_ofs: u64,
    state: ZipInternalState,
    last_error: Option<ZipError>,
    zip_type: ZipType,
    zip_mode: ZipMode,
}

impl<R: Read + Seek> ZipArchive<R> {
    pub fn new(reader: R, size: u64, flags: u32) -> ZipResult<Self> {
        let mut archive = Self {
            reader,
            archive_size: size,
            total_files: 0,
            central_directory_file_ofs: 0,
            state: ZipInternalState::new(),
            last_error: None,
            zip_type: ZipType::User,
            zip_mode: ZipMode::Reading,
        };

        archive.state.init_flags = flags;
        
        if !archive.read_central_dir(flags)? {
            return Err(archive.last_error.unwrap_or(ZipError::UnknownError));
        }

        Ok(archive)
    }

    fn set_error(&mut self, error: ZipError) -> bool {
        self.last_error = Some(error);
        false
    }

    fn locate_header_sig(&mut self, sig: u32, header_size: usize) -> ZipResult<Option<u64>> {
        if self.archive_size < header_size as u64 {
            return Ok(None);
        }

        let mut buf = [0u8; 4];
        let mut pos = self.archive_size.saturating_sub(65536 + header_size as u64);
        if pos > self.archive_size {
            pos = 0;
        }

        while pos <= self.archive_size.saturating_sub(header_size as u64) {
            self.reader.seek(SeekFrom::Start(pos))?;
            self.reader.read_exact(&mut buf)?;

            let read_sig = u32::from_le_bytes(buf);
            if read_sig == sig {
                return Ok(Some(pos));
            }
            pos += 1;
        }

        Ok(None)
    }

    fn read_exact_at(&mut self, pos: u64, buf: &mut [u8]) -> ZipResult<()> {
        self.reader.seek(SeekFrom::Start(pos))?;
        self.reader.read_exact(buf)?;
        Ok(())
    }

    fn read_central_dir(&mut self, flags: u32) -> ZipResult<bool> {
        let sort_central_dir = (flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_DO_NOT_SORT_CENTRAL_DIRECTORY) == 0;

        // Basic sanity checks
        if self.archive_size < MZ_ZIP_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as u64 {
            return Ok(self.set_error(ZipError::NotAnArchive));
        }

        // Locate EOCD
        let eocd_ofs = match self.locate_header_sig(MZ_ZIP_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIG, MZ_ZIP_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE)? {
            Some(ofs) => ofs,
            None => return Ok(self.set_error(ZipError::FailedFindingCentralDir)),
        };

        // Read EOCD
        let mut eocd_buf = [0u8; MZ_ZIP_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE];
        self.read_exact_at(eocd_ofs, &mut eocd_buf)?;

        if u32::from_le_bytes(eocd_buf[MZ_ZIP_ECDH_SIG_OFS..MZ_ZIP_ECDH_SIG_OFS+4].try_into().unwrap()) != MZ_ZIP_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIG {
            return Ok(self.set_error(ZipError::NotAnArchive));
        }

        // Check for ZIP64 locator
        if eocd_ofs >= (MZ_ZIP64_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_LOCATOR_SIZE + MZ_ZIP64_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE) as u64 {
            let locator_ofs = eocd_ofs - MZ_ZIP64_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_LOCATOR_SIZE as u64;
            let mut locator_buf = [0u8; MZ_ZIP64_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_LOCATOR_SIZE];
            if self.read_exact_at(locator_ofs, &mut locator_buf).is_ok() {
                if u32::from_le_bytes(locator_buf[MZ_ZIP64_ECDL_SIG_OFS..MZ_ZIP64_ECDL_SIG_OFS+4].try_into().unwrap()) == MZ_ZIP64_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_LOCATOR_SIG {
                    self.state.zip64 = true;
                }
            }
        }

        // Parse EOCD fields
        self.total_files = u16::from_le_bytes(eocd_buf[MZ_ZIP_ECDH_CDIR_TOTAL_ENTRIES_OFS..MZ_ZIP_ECDH_CDIR_TOTAL_ENTRIES_OFS+2].try_into().unwrap()) as u32;
        let cdir_entries_on_this_disk = u16::from_le_bytes(eocd_buf[MZ_ZIP_ECDH_CDIR_NUM_ENTRIES_ON_DISK_OFS..MZ_ZIP_ECDH_CDIR_NUM_ENTRIES_ON_DISK_OFS+2].try_into().unwrap()) as u32;
        let num_this_disk = u16::from_le_bytes(eocd_buf[MZ_ZIP_ECDH_NUM_THIS_DISK_OFS..MZ_ZIP_ECDH_NUM_THIS_DISK_OFS+2].try_into().unwrap()) as u32;
        let cdir_disk_index = u16::from_le_bytes(eocd_buf[MZ_ZIP_ECDH_NUM_DISK_CDIR_OFS..MZ_ZIP_ECDH_NUM_DISK_CDIR_OFS+2].try_into().unwrap()) as u32;
        let mut cdir_size = u32::from_le_bytes(eocd_buf[MZ_ZIP_ECDH_CDIR_SIZE_OFS..MZ_ZIP_ECDH_CDIR_SIZE_OFS+4].try_into().unwrap()) as u64;
        let mut cdir_ofs = u32::from_le_bytes(eocd_buf[MZ_ZIP_ECDH_CDIR_OFS_OFS..MZ_ZIP_ECDH_CDIR_OFS_OFS+4].try_into().unwrap()) as u64;

        // Handle ZIP64 if needed
        if self.state.zip64 {
            // Read ZIP64 EOCD locator and directory
            let zip64_eocd_ofs = eocd_ofs - MZ_ZIP64_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_LOCATOR_SIZE as u64 - MZ_ZIP64_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as u64;
            let mut zip64_eocd_buf = [0u8; MZ_ZIP64_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE];
            
            if self.read_exact_at(zip64_eocd_ofs, &mut zip64_eocd_buf).is_ok() &&
               u32::from_le_bytes(zip64_eocd_buf[MZ_ZIP64_ECDH_SIG_OFS..MZ_ZIP64_ECDH_SIG_OFS+4].try_into().unwrap()) == MZ_ZIP64_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIG {
                
                let zip64_cdir_total_entries = u64::from_le_bytes(zip64_eocd_buf[MZ_ZIP64_ECDH_CDIR_TOTAL_ENTRIES_OFS..MZ_ZIP64_ECDH_CDIR_TOTAL_ENTRIES_OFS+8].try_into().unwrap());
                let zip64_cdir_total_entries_on_this_disk = u64::from_le_bytes(zip64_eocd_buf[MZ_ZIP64_ECDH_CDIR_NUM_ENTRIES_ON_DISK_OFS..MZ_ZIP64_ECDH_CDIR_NUM_ENTRIES_ON_DISK_OFS+8].try_into().unwrap());
                let zip64_size_of_central_directory = u64::from_le_bytes(zip64_eocd_buf[MZ_ZIP64_ECDH_CDIR_SIZE_OFS..MZ_ZIP64_ECDH_CDIR_SIZE_OFS+8].try_into().unwrap());

                if zip64_cdir_total_entries > u32::MAX as u64 {
                    return Ok(self.set_error(ZipError::TooManyFiles));
                }
                self.total_files = zip64_cdir_total_entries as u32;

                if zip64_cdir_total_entries_on_this_disk > u32::MAX as u64 {
                    return Ok(self.set_error(ZipError::TooManyFiles));
                }

                if zip64_size_of_central_directory > u32::MAX as u64 {
                    return Ok(self.set_error(ZipError::UnsupportedCdirSize));
                }
                cdir_size = zip64_size_of_central_directory;

                cdir_ofs = u64::from_le_bytes(zip64_eocd_buf[MZ_ZIP64_ECDH_CDIR_OFS_OFS..MZ_ZIP64_ECDH_CDIR_OFS_OFS+8].try_into().unwrap());
            }
        }

        // Validate
        if self.total_files != cdir_entries_on_this_disk {
            return Ok(self.set_error(ZipError::UnsupportedMultidisk));
        }

        if ((num_this_disk | cdir_disk_index) != 0) && ((num_this_disk != 1) || (cdir_disk_index != 1)) {
            return Ok(self.set_error(ZipError::UnsupportedMultidisk));
        }

        if cdir_size < self.total_files as u64 * MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as u64 {
            return Ok(self.set_error(ZipError::InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted));
        }

        if (cdir_ofs + cdir_size) > self.archive_size {
            return Ok(self.set_error(ZipError::InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted));
        }

        if eocd_ofs < cdir_ofs + cdir_size {
            return Ok(self.set_error(ZipError::InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted));
        }

        self.central_directory_file_ofs = cdir_ofs;

        // Read central directory
        if self.total_files > 0 {
            // Allocate buffers
            self.state.central_dir.resize(cdir_size as usize, false)?;
            self.state.central_dir_offsets.resize(self.total_files as usize, false)?;
            
            if sort_central_dir {
                self.state.sorted_central_dir_offsets.resize(self.total_files as usize, false)?;
            }

            // Read central directory data
            self.read_exact_at(cdir_ofs, self.state.central_dir.as_mut_slice())?;

            // Parse central directory entries
            let mut p = 0;
            for i in 0..self.total_files as usize {
                if p + MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE > self.state.central_dir.len() {
                    return Ok(self.set_error(ZipError::InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted));
                }

                let sig = u32::from_le_bytes(self.state.central_dir.as_slice()[p+MZ_ZIP_CDH_SIG_OFS..p+MZ_ZIP_CDH_SIG_OFS+4].try_into().unwrap());
                if sig != MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIG {
                    return Ok(self.set_error(ZipError::InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted));
                }

                self.state.central_dir_offsets.as_mut_slice()[i] = p as u32;
                if sort_central_dir {
                    self.state.sorted_central_dir_offsets.as_mut_slice()[i] = i as u32;
                }

                let comp_size = u32::from_le_bytes(self.state.central_dir.as_slice()[p+MZ_ZIP_CDH_COMPRESSED_SIZE_OFS..p+MZ_ZIP_CDH_COMPRESSED_SIZE_OFS+4].try_into().unwrap());
                let decomp_size = u32::from_le_bytes(self.state.central_dir.as_slice()[p+MZ_ZIP_CDH_DECOMPRESSED_SIZE_OFS..p+MZ_ZIP_CDH_DECOMPRESSED_SIZE_OFS+4].try_into().unwrap());
                let local_header_ofs = u32::from_le_bytes(self.state.central_dir.as_slice()[p+MZ_ZIP_CDH_LOCAL_HEADER_OFS..p+MZ_ZIP_CDH_LOCAL_HEADER_OFS+4].try_into().unwrap());
                let filename_size = u16::from_le_bytes(self.state.central_dir.as_slice()[p+MZ_ZIP_CDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS..p+MZ_ZIP_CDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS+2].try_into().unwrap()) as usize;
                let ext_data_size = u16::from_le_bytes(self.state.central_dir.as_slice()[p+MZ_ZIP_CDH_EXTRA_LEN_OFS..p+MZ_ZIP_CDH_EXTRA_LEN_OFS+2].try_into().unwrap()) as usize;
                let comment_size = u16::from_le_bytes(self.state.central_dir.as_slice()[p+MZ_ZIP_CDH_COMMENT_LEN_OFS..p+MZ_ZIP_CDH_COMMENT_LEN_OFS+2].try_into().unwrap()) as usize;

                // Check for ZIP64 extended info
                if !self.state.zip64_has_extended_info_fields && ext_data_size > 0 &&
                   (comp_size == MZ_UINT32_MAX || decomp_size == MZ_UINT32_MAX || local_header_ofs == MZ_UINT32_MAX) {
                    
                    let extra_start = p + MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE + filename_size;
                    let mut extra_pos = 0;
                    
                    while extra_pos + 4 <= ext_data_size {
                        let field_id = u16::from_le_bytes(self.state.central_dir.as_slice()[extra_start+extra_pos..extra_start+extra_pos+2].try_into().unwrap());
                        let field_data_size = u16::from_le_bytes(self.state.central_dir.as_slice()[extra_start+extra_pos+2..extra_start+extra_pos+4].try_into().unwrap()) as usize;
                        
                        if field_id == MZ_ZIP64_EXTENDED_INFORMATION_FIELD_HEADER_ID {
                            self.state.zip64 = true;
                            self.state.zip64_has_extended_info_fields = true;
                            break;
                        }
                        
                        extra_pos += 4 + field_data_size;
                    }
                }

                // Validate compression
                let method = u16::from_le_bytes(self.state.central_dir.as_slice()[p+MZ_ZIP_CDH_METHOD_OFS..p+MZ_ZIP_CDH_METHOD_OFS+2].try_into().unwrap());
                if method == 0 && decomp_size != comp_size {
                    return Ok(self.set_error(ZipError::InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted));
                }

                // Check disk index
                let disk_index = u16::from_le_bytes(self.state.central_dir.as_slice()[p+MZ_ZIP_CDH_DISK_START_OFS..p+MZ_ZIP_CDH_DISK_START_OFS+2].try_into().unwrap());
                if disk_index == MZ_UINT16_MAX || (disk_index != num_this_disk as u16 && disk_index != 1) {
                    return Ok(self.set_error(ZipError::UnsupportedMultidisk));
                }

                // Check bit flags
                let bit_flags = u16::from_le_bytes(self.state.central_dir.as_slice()[p+MZ_ZIP_CDH_BIT_FLAG_OFS..p+MZ_ZIP_CDH_BIT_FLAG_OFS+2].try_into().unwrap());
                if bit_flags & MZ_ZIP_GENERAL_PURPOSE_BIT_FLAG_LOCAL_DIR_IS_MASKED != 0 {
                    return Ok(self.set_error(ZipError::UnsupportedEncryption));
                }

                let total_header_size = MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE + filename_size + ext_data_size + comment_size;
                if total_header_size > self.state.central_dir.len() - p {
                    return Ok(self.set_error(ZipError::InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted));
                }

                p += total_header_size;
            }
        }

        // Sort central directory if requested
        if sort_central_dir {
            // Simple bubble sort for demonstration (C code uses heap sort)
            let mut offsets = self.state.sorted_central_dir_offsets.as_mut_slice().to_vec();
            let dir_data = self.state.central_dir.as_slice();
            
            for i in 0..offsets.len() {
                for j in 0..offsets.len() - i - 1 {
                    let idx1 = offsets[j] as usize;
                    let idx2 = offsets[j + 1] as usize;
                    
                    let ofs1 = self.state.central_dir_offsets.as_slice()[idx1] as usize;
                    let ofs2 = self.state.central_dir_offsets.as_slice()[idx2] as usize;
                    
                    let name_len1 = u16::from_le_bytes(dir_data[ofs1+MZ_ZIP_CDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS..ofs1+MZ_ZIP_CDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS+2].try_into().unwrap()) as usize;
                    let name_len2 = u16::from_le_bytes(dir_data[ofs2+MZ_ZIP_CDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS..ofs2+MZ_ZIP_CDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS+2].try_into().unwrap()) as usize;
                    
                    let name1 = &dir_data[ofs1+MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE..ofs1+MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE+name_len1];
                    let name2 = &dir_data[ofs2+MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE..ofs2+MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE+name_len2];
                    
                    if name1 > name2 {
                        offsets.swap(j, j + 1);
                    }
                }
            }
            
            for (i, &val) in offsets.iter().enumerate() {
                self.state.sorted_central_dir_offsets.as_mut_slice()[i] = val;
            }
        }

        Ok(true)
    }

    pub fn end_internal(&mut self, set_last_error: bool) -> bool {
        if self.zip_mode != ZipMode::Reading {
            if set_last_error {
                self.last_error = Some(ZipError::InvalidParameter);
            }
            return false;
        }

        // Clear arrays
        self.state.central_dir.clear();
        self.state.central_dir_offsets.clear();
        self.state.sorted_central_dir_offsets.clear();

        // Close file if open
        if let Some(file) = &mut self.state.file.take() {
            drop(file);
        }

        self.zip_mode = ZipMode::Invalid;
        true
    }

    pub fn end(&mut self) -> bool {
        self.end_internal(true)
    }

    pub fn get_cdh(&self, file_index: u32) -> Option<&[u8]> {
        if file_index >= self.total_files {
            return None;
        }
        
        let ofs = self.state.central_dir_offsets.as_slice()[file_index as usize] as usize;
        if ofs + MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE > self.state.central_dir.len() {
            return None;
        }
        
        Some(&self.state.central_dir.as_slice()[ofs..])
    }

    pub fn is_file_encrypted(&self, file_index: u32) -> ZipResult<bool> {
        let cdh = self.get_cdh(file_index).ok_or(ZipError::InvalidParameter)?;
        
        let bit_flag = u16::from_le_bytes(cdh[MZ_ZIP_CDH_BIT_FLAG_OFS..MZ_ZIP_CDH_BIT_FLAG_OFS+2].try_into().unwrap());
        Ok(bit_flag & (MZ_ZIP_GENERAL_PURPOSE_BIT_FLAG_IS_ENCRYPTED | MZ_ZIP_GENERAL_PURPOSE_BIT_FLAG_USES_STRONG_ENCRYPTION) != 0)
    }

    pub fn is_file_supported(&self, file_index: u32) -> ZipResult<bool> {
        let cdh = self.get_cdh(file_index).ok_or(ZipError::InvalidParameter)?;
        
        let method = u16::from_le_bytes(cdh[MZ_ZIP_CDH_METHOD_OFS..MZ_ZIP_CDH_METHOD_OFS+2].try_into().unwrap());
        let bit_flag = u16::from_le_bytes(cdh[MZ_ZIP_CDH_BIT_FLAG_OFS..MZ_ZIP_CDH_BIT_FLAG_OFS+2].try_into().unwrap());

        if method != 0 && method != 8 { // 8 = DEFLATED
            return Err(ZipError::UnsupportedMethod);
        }

        if bit_flag & (MZ_ZIP_GENERAL_PURPOSE_BIT_FLAG_IS_ENCRYPTED | MZ_ZIP_GENERAL_PURPOSE_BIT_FLAG_USES_STRONG_ENCRYPTION) != 0 {
            return Err(ZipError::UnsupportedEncryption);
        }

        if bit_flag & MZ_ZIP_GENERAL_PURPOSE_BIT_FLAG_COMPRESSED_PATCH_FLAG != 0 {
            return Err(ZipError::UnsupportedFeature);
        }

        Ok(true)
    }
}

// Memory-based zip archive
impl ZipArchive<std::io::Cursor<Vec<u8>>> {
    pub fn init_mem(data: Vec<u8>, flags: u32) -> ZipResult<Self> {
        if data.len() < MZ_ZIP_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE {
            return Err(ZipError::ArchiveTooSmall);
        }

        let cursor = std::io::Cursor::new(data);
        ZipArchive::new(cursor, cursor.get_ref().len() as u64, flags)
    }
}

// File-based zip archive
impl ZipArchive<File> {
    pub fn init_file<P: AsRef<Path>>(filename: P, flags: u32) -> ZipResult<Self> {
        let mut file = File::open(filename).map_err(|_| ZipError::FileOpenFailed)?;
        
        let file_size = file.seek(SeekFrom::End(0)).map_err(|_| ZipError::FileSeekFailed)?;
        file.seek(SeekFrom::Start(0)).map_err(|_| ZipError::FileSeekFailed)?;
        
        if file_size < MZ_ZIP_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as u64 {
            return Err(ZipError::ArchiveTooSmall);
        }

        let mut archive = ZipArchive::new(file, file_size, flags)?;
        archive.zip_type = ZipType::File;
        Ok(archive)
    }

    pub fn init_file_v2<P: AsRef<Path>>(filename: P, flags: u32, file_start_ofs: u64, archive_size: u64) -> ZipResult<Self> {
        let mut file = File::open(filename).map_err(|_| ZipError::FileOpenFailed)?;
        
        let file_size = if archive_size == 0 {
            file.seek(SeekFrom::End(0)).map_err(|_| ZipError::FileSeekFailed)? - file_start_ofs
        } else {
            archive_size
        };
        
        file.seek(SeekFrom::Start(file_start_ofs)).map_err(|_| ZipError::FileSeekFailed)?;
        
        if file_size < MZ_ZIP_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as u64 {
            return Err(ZipError::ArchiveTooSmall);
        }

        let mut archive = ZipArchive::new(file, file_size, flags)?;
        archive.zip_type = ZipType::File;
        archive.state.file_archive_start_ofs = file_start_ofs;
        Ok(archive)
    }
}

// Zero struct helper
pub fn zero_struct<T>(obj: &mut T) {
    unsafe {
        ptr::write_bytes(obj as *mut T, 0, 1);
    }
}

// --- Module: mz_p3 ---
//! ZIP archive reader functions (safe Rust translation)


/// Error types for ZIP operations
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]

impl std::fmt::Display for ZipError {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        write!(f, "{:?}", self)
    }
}

impl std::error::Error for ZipError {}

/// Type alias for result with ZipError

/// ZIP archive reader struct (simplified from C)

/// Internal state of ZIP archive

/// File statistics
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct ZipFileStat {
    pub file_index: u32,
    pub central_dir_ofs: u32,
    pub version_made_by: u16,
    pub version_needed: u16,
    pub bit_flag: u16,
    pub method: u16,
    pub time: u64, // Use u64 for timestamp
    pub crc32: u32,
    pub comp_size: u64,
    pub uncomp_size: u64,
    pub internal_attr: u16,
    pub external_attr: u32,
    pub local_header_ofs: u64,
    pub filename: String,
    pub comment: String,
    pub comment_size: u16,
    pub is_directory: bool,
    pub is_encrypted: bool,
    pub is_supported: bool,
}

impl ZipArchive {
    /// Check if file at given index is a directory
    pub fn is_file_a_directory(&self, file_index: u32) -> ZipResult<bool> {
        let state = self.state.as_ref().ok_or(ZipError::InvalidParameter)?;
        
        let cdh = self.get_cdh(file_index).ok_or(ZipError::InvalidParameter)?;
        
        let filename_len = u16::from_le_bytes([cdh[28], cdh[29]]) as usize;
        
        // Check if filename ends with '/'
        if filename_len > 0 {
            let filename_start = 46; // MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE
            if cdh[filename_start + filename_len - 1] == b'/' {
                return Ok(true);
            }
        }
        
        // Check DOS directory attribute
        let external_attr = u32::from_le_bytes([
            cdh[38], cdh[39], cdh[40], cdh[41]
        ]);
        
        if (external_attr & 0x10) != 0 {
            Ok(true)
        } else {
            Ok(false)
        }
    }
    
    /// Get central directory header for file index
    fn get_cdh(&self, file_index: u32) -> Option<&[u8]> {
        let state = self.state.as_ref()?;
        if file_index as usize >= state.central_dir_offsets.len() {
            return None;
        }
        
        let offset = state.central_dir_offsets[file_index as usize] as usize;
        if offset + 46 > state.central_dir.len() {
            return None;
        }
        
        Some(&state.central_dir[offset..offset + 46])
    }
    
    /// Get file statistics (internal version)
    fn file_stat_internal(
        &self,
        file_index: u32,
        central_dir_header: &[u8],
        found_zip64_extra_data: &mut bool,
    ) -> ZipResult<ZipFileStat> {
        if central_dir_header.len() < 46 {
            return Err(ZipError::InvalidParameter);
        }
        
        let mut stat = ZipFileStat {
            file_index,
            central_dir_ofs: self.state.as_ref()
                .and_then(|s| s.central_dir_offsets.get(file_index as usize).copied())
                .unwrap_or(0),
            version_made_by: u16::from_le_bytes([central_dir_header[4], central_dir_header[5]]),
            version_needed: u16::from_le_bytes([central_dir_header[6], central_dir_header[7]]),
            bit_flag: u16::from_le_bytes([central_dir_header[8], central_dir_header[9]]),
            method: u16::from_le_bytes([central_dir_header[10], central_dir_header[11]]),
            time: 0, // Simplified - would need dos_to_time_t conversion
            crc32: u32::from_le_bytes([
                central_dir_header[16], central_dir_header[17],
                central_dir_header[18], central_dir_header[19]
            ]),
            comp_size: u32::from_le_bytes([
                central_dir_header[20], central_dir_header[21],
                central_dir_header[22], central_dir_header[23]
            ]) as u64,
            uncomp_size: u32::from_le_bytes([
                central_dir_header[24], central_dir_header[25],
                central_dir_header[26], central_dir_header[27]
            ]) as u64,
            internal_attr: u16::from_le_bytes([central_dir_header[36], central_dir_header[37]]),
            external_attr: u32::from_le_bytes([
                central_dir_header[38], central_dir_header[39],
                central_dir_header[40], central_dir_header[41]
            ]),
            local_header_ofs: u32::from_le_bytes([
                central_dir_header[42], central_dir_header[43],
                central_dir_header[44], central_dir_header[45]
            ]) as u64,
            filename: String::new(),
            comment: String::new(),
            comment_size: 0,
            is_directory: false,
            is_encrypted: false,
            is_supported: false,
        };
        
        // Read filename
        let filename_len = u16::from_le_bytes([central_dir_header[28], central_dir_header[29]]) as usize;
        let max_len = std::cmp::min(filename_len, 256 - 1); // MZ_ZIP_MAX_ARCHIVE_FILENAME_SIZE
        if central_dir_header.len() >= 46 + max_len {
            stat.filename = String::from_utf8_lossy(&central_dir_header[46..46 + max_len]).into_owned();
        }
        
        // Read comment
        let extra_len = u16::from_le_bytes([central_dir_header[30], central_dir_header[31]]) as usize;
        let comment_len = u16::from_le_bytes([central_dir_header[32], central_dir_header[33]]) as usize;
        let max_comment_len = std::cmp::min(comment_len, 256 - 1); // MZ_ZIP_MAX_ARCHIVE_FILE_COMMENT_SIZE
        
        let comment_start = 46 + filename_len + extra_len;
        if central_dir_header.len() >= comment_start + max_comment_len {
            stat.comment = String::from_utf8_lossy(
                &central_dir_header[comment_start..comment_start + max_comment_len]
            ).into_owned();
        }
        stat.comment_size = max_comment_len as u16;
        
        // Set flags
        stat.is_directory = self.is_file_a_directory(file_index).unwrap_or(false);
        // Note: is_encrypted and is_supported would need additional functions
        
        // Check for ZIP64 extended information
        *found_zip64_extra_data = false;
        
        if stat.comp_size == 0xFFFFFFFF ||
           stat.uncomp_size == 0xFFFFFFFF ||
           stat.local_header_ofs == 0xFFFFFFFF
        {
            let extra_len = u16::from_le_bytes([central_dir_header[30], central_dir_header[31]]) as usize;
            let mut extra_data = &central_dir_header[46 + filename_len..];
            
            while extra_data.len() >= 4 {
                let field_id = u16::from_le_bytes([extra_data[0], extra_data[1]]);
                let field_size = u16::from_le_bytes([extra_data[2], extra_data[3]]) as usize;
                
                if extra_data.len() < 4 + field_size {
                    break;
                }
                
                if field_id == 0x0001 { // MZ_ZIP64_EXTENDED_INFORMATION_FIELD_HEADER_ID
                    *found_zip64_extra_data = true;
                    let mut field = &extra_data[4..];
                    let mut remaining = field_size;
                    
                    if stat.uncomp_size == 0xFFFFFFFF && remaining >= 8 {
                        stat.uncomp_size = u64::from_le_bytes([
                            field[0], field[1], field[2], field[3],
                            field[4], field[5], field[6], field[7]
                        ]);
                        field = &field[8..];
                        remaining -= 8;
                    }
                    
                    if stat.comp_size == 0xFFFFFFFF && remaining >= 8 {
                        stat.comp_size = u64::from_le_bytes([
                            field[0], field[1], field[2], field[3],
                            field[4], field[5], field[6], field[7]
                        ]);
                        field = &field[8..];
                        remaining -= 8;
                    }
                    
                    if stat.local_header_ofs == 0xFFFFFFFF && remaining >= 8 {
                        stat.local_header_ofs = u64::from_le_bytes([
                            field[0], field[1], field[2], field[3],
                            field[4], field[5], field[6], field[7]
                        ]);
                    }
                    
                    break;
                }
                
                // Move to next field
                let skip = 4 + field_size;
                if extra_data.len() > skip {
                    extra_data = &extra_data[skip..];
                } else {
                    break;
                }
            }
        }
        
        Ok(stat)
    }
    
    /// Compare strings with case sensitivity option
    fn string_equal(a: &str, b: &str, flags: u32) -> bool {
        if flags & 0x1000 != 0 { // MZ_ZIP_FLAG_CASE_SENSITIVE
            a == b
        } else {
            a.eq_ignore_ascii_case(b)
        }
    }
    
    /// Compare filename for binary search
    fn filename_compare(
        &self,
        l_index: u32,
        r_filename: &str,
    ) -> ZipResult<i32> {
        let state = self.state.as_ref().ok_or(ZipError::InvalidParameter)?;
        
        let cdh = self.get_cdh(l_index).ok_or(ZipError::InvalidParameter)?;
        let filename_len = u16::from_le_bytes([cdh[28], cdh[29]]) as usize;
        
        let filename_start = 46; // MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE
        if cdh.len() < filename_start + filename_len {
            return Err(ZipError::InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted);
        }
        
        let l_filename_bytes = &cdh[filename_start..filename_start + filename_len];
        let l_filename = String::from_utf8_lossy(l_filename_bytes);
        
        let min_len = std::cmp::min(l_filename.len(), r_filename.len());
        
        for i in 0..min_len {
            let lc = l_filename.as_bytes()[i].to_ascii_lowercase();
            let rc = r_filename.as_bytes()[i].to_ascii_lowercase();
            
            if lc != rc {
                return Ok(lc as i32 - rc as i32);
            }
        }
        
        Ok(l_filename.len() as i32 - r_filename.len() as i32)
    }
    
    /// Locate file using binary search
    fn locate_file_binary_search(&self, filename: &str) -> ZipResult<u32> {
        let state = self.state.as_ref().ok_or(ZipError::InvalidParameter)?;
        
        if state.sorted_central_dir_offsets.is_empty() {
            return Err(ZipError::FileNotFound);
        }
        
        let mut low = 0;
        let mut high = state.sorted_central_dir_offsets.len() - 1;
        
        while low <= high {
            let mid = low + (high - low) / 2;
            let file_index = state.sorted_central_dir_offsets[mid];
            
            match self.filename_compare(file_index, filename)? {
                0 => return Ok(file_index),
                cmp if cmp < 0 => low = mid + 1,
                _ => high = mid - 1,
            }
        }
        
        Err(ZipError::FileNotFound)
    }
    
    /// Locate file with flags and optional comment
    pub fn locate_file_v2(
        &self,
        name: &str,
        comment: Option<&str>,
        flags: u32,
    ) -> ZipResult<u32> {
        let state = self.state.as_ref().ok_or(ZipError::InvalidParameter)?;
        
        // Try binary search if conditions are right
        if (state.init_flags & 0x800) == 0 && // MZ_ZIP_FLAG_DO_NOT_SORT_CENTRAL_DIRECTORY
           self.zip_mode == 0 && // MZ_ZIP_MODE_READING
           (flags & (0x800 | 0x1000)) == 0 && // MZ_ZIP_FLAG_IGNORE_PATH | MZ_ZIP_FLAG_CASE_SENSITIVE
           comment.is_none() &&
           !state.sorted_central_dir_offsets.is_empty()
        {
            return self.locate_file_binary_search(name);
        }
        
        // Linear scan
        let name_len = name.len();
        if name_len > 0xFFFF {
            return Err(ZipError::InvalidParameter);
        }
        
        let comment_len = comment.map(|c| c.len()).unwrap_or(0);
        if comment_len > 0xFFFF {
            return Err(ZipError::InvalidParameter);
        }
        
        for file_index in 0..self.total_files {
            let cdh = match self.get_cdh(file_index) {
                Some(cdh) => cdh,
                None => continue,
            };
            
            let filename_len = u16::from_le_bytes([cdh[28], cdh[29]]) as usize;
            if filename_len < name_len {
                continue;
            }
            
            // Check comment if provided
            if let Some(comment_str) = comment {
                let extra_len = u16::from_le_bytes([cdh[30], cdh[31]]) as usize;
                let file_comment_len = u16::from_le_bytes([cdh[32], cdh[33]]) as usize;
                
                let comment_start = 46 + filename_len + extra_len;
                if cdh.len() < comment_start + file_comment_len {
                    continue;
                }
                
                let file_comment = String::from_utf8_lossy(
                    &cdh[comment_start..comment_start + file_comment_len]
                );
                
                if file_comment_len != comment_len ||
                   !self.string_equal(comment_str, &file_comment, flags)
                {
                    continue;
                }
            }
            
            // Handle ignore path flag
            let mut filename = &cdh[46..46 + filename_len];
            let mut actual_len = filename_len;
            
            if flags & 0x800 != 0 && filename_len > 0 { // MZ_ZIP_FLAG_IGNORE_PATH
                for i in (0..filename_len).rev() {
                    let ch = filename[i];
                    if ch == b'/' || ch == b'\\' || ch == b':' {
                        filename = &filename[i + 1..];
                        actual_len = filename_len - (i + 1);
                        break;
                    }
                }
            }
            
            let filename_str = String::from_utf8_lossy(filename);
            if actual_len == name_len && self.string_equal(name, &filename_str, flags) {
                return Ok(file_index);
            }
        }
        
        Err(ZipError::FileNotFound)
    }
    
    /// Locate file (legacy version returning i32)
    pub fn locate_file(
        &self,
        name: &str,
        comment: Option<&str>,
        flags: u32,
    ) -> i32 {
        match self.locate_file_v2(name, comment, flags) {
            Ok(index) => index as i32,
            Err(_) => -1,
        }
    }
    
    /// Extract file to memory (internal)
    fn extract_to_mem_no_alloc1(
        &self,
        file_index: u32,
        buf: &mut [u8],
        flags: u32,
        user_read_buf: Option<&mut [u8]>,
        stat: Option<&ZipFileStat>,
    ) -> ZipResult<()> {
        // Get file statistics
        let file_stat = match stat {
            Some(s) => s.clone(),
            None => {
                let cdh = self.get_cdh(file_index).ok_or(ZipError::InvalidParameter)?;
                let mut found_zip64 = false;
                self.file_stat_internal(file_index, cdh, &mut found_zip64)?
            }
        };
        
        // Directory or zero length
        if file_stat.is_directory || file_stat.uncomp_size == 0 {
            return Ok(());
        }
        
        // Check encryption
        if file_stat.bit_flag & (1 | 64 | 32) != 0 {
            return Err(ZipError::UnsupportedEncryption);
        }
        
        // Check compression method
        if (flags & 0x40000) == 0 && // MZ_ZIP_FLAG_COMPRESSED_DATA
           file_stat.method != 0 && file_stat.method != 8 // MZ_DEFLATED
        {
            return Err(ZipError::UnsupportedMethod);
        }
        
        // Check buffer size
        let needed_size = if flags & 0x40000 != 0 {
            file_stat.comp_size
        } else {
            file_stat.uncomp_size
        };
        
        if buf.len() < needed_size as usize {
            return Err(ZipError::BufTooSmall);
        }
        
        // Read local header
        let mut local_header = [0u8; 30];
        (self.reader)(file_stat.local_header_ofs, &mut local_header)
            .map_err(|_| ZipError::FileReadFailed)?;
        
        if u32::from_le_bytes([local_header[0], local_header[1], local_header[2], local_header[3]]) 
            != 0x04034b50 // MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIG
        {
            return Err(ZipError::InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted);
        }
        
        let filename_len = u16::from_le_bytes([local_header[26], local_header[27]]) as u64;
        let extra_len = u16::from_le_bytes([local_header[28], local_header[29]]) as u64;
        let data_offset = file_stat.local_header_ofs + 30 + filename_len + extra_len;
        
        if data_offset + file_stat.comp_size > self.archive_size {
            return Err(ZipError::InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted);
        }
        
        // Stored data or compressed data requested
        if (flags & 0x40000) != 0 || file_stat.method == 0 {
            (self.reader)(data_offset, &mut buf[..needed_size as usize])
                .map_err(|_| ZipError::FileReadFailed)?;
            
            // Verify CRC for uncompressed data
            if (flags & 0x40000) == 0 {
                // Note: CRC check would go here
                // if mz_crc32(buf, file_stat.uncomp_size as usize) != file_stat.crc32 {
                //     return Err(ZipError::CrcCheckFailed);
                // }
            }
            
            return Ok(());
        }
        
        // Decompression needed
        // Note: Decompressor implementation would go here
        // This is simplified - actual implementation would use inflate
        
        Err(ZipError::UnsupportedMethod) // Simplified
    }
    
    /// Extract file to memory without allocation
    pub fn extract_to_mem_no_alloc(
        &self,
        file_index: u32,
        buf: &mut [u8],
        flags: u32,
        user_read_buf: Option<&mut [u8]>,
    ) -> ZipResult<()> {
        self.extract_to_mem_no_alloc1(file_index, buf, flags, user_read_buf, None)
    }
    
    /// Extract file by name to memory without allocation
    pub fn extract_file_to_mem_no_alloc(
        &self,
        filename: &str,
        buf: &mut [u8],
        flags: u32,
        user_read_buf: Option<&mut [u8]>,
    ) -> ZipResult<()> {
        let file_index = self.locate_file_v2(filename, None, flags)?;
        self.extract_to_mem_no_alloc1(file_index, buf, flags, user_read_buf, None)
    }
    
    /// Extract file to memory (simple version)
    pub fn extract_to_mem(
        &self,
        file_index: u32,
        buf: &mut [u8],
        flags: u32,
    ) -> ZipResult<()> {
        self.extract_to_mem_no_alloc1(file_index, buf, flags, None, None)
    }
    
    /// Extract file by name to memory
    pub fn extract_file_to_mem(
        &self,
        filename: &str,
        buf: &mut [u8],
        flags: u32,
    ) -> ZipResult<()> {
        let file_index = self.locate_file_v2(filename, None, flags)?;
        self.extract_to_mem_no_alloc1(file_index, buf, flags, None, None)
    }
    
    /// Extract file to heap-allocated buffer
    pub fn extract_to_heap(
        &self,
        file_index: u32,
        flags: u32,
    ) -> ZipResult<Vec<u8>> {
        // Get file statistics
        let cdh = self.get_cdh(file_index).ok_or(ZipError::InvalidParameter)?;
        let mut found_zip64 = false;
        let stat = self.file_stat_internal(file_index, cdh, &mut found_zip64)?;
        
        let alloc_size = if flags & 0x40000 != 0 {
            stat.comp_size as usize
        } else {
            stat.uncomp_size as usize
        };
        
        let mut buf = vec![0u8; alloc_size];
        
        self.extract_to_mem_no_alloc1(file_index, &mut buf, flags, None, Some(&stat))?;
        
        Ok(buf)
    }
    
    /// Extract file by name to heap
    pub fn extract_file_to_heap(
        &self,
        filename: &str,
        flags: u32,
    ) -> ZipResult<Vec<u8>> {
        let file_index = self.locate_file_v2(filename, None, flags)?;
        self.extract_to_heap(file_index, flags)
    }
}

// Helper function for case-insensitive ASCII comparison
fn ascii_tolower(c: u8) -> u8 {
    if c >= b'A' && c <= b'Z' {
        c - b'A' + b'a'
    } else {
        c
    }
}

// --- Module: mz_p4 ---
// Constants from the original C code
const MZ_ZIP_LDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS: usize = 26;
const MZ_ZIP_LDH_EXTRA_LEN_OFS: usize = 28;
const MZ_DEFLATED: u16 = 8;
const MZ_ZIP_FLAG_COMPRESSED_DATA: u32 = 0x80000;
const MZ_ZIP_MAX_IO_BUF_SIZE: u64 = 1024 * 1024;
const TINFL_LZ_DICT_SIZE: usize = 32768;

// Error types
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]

impl From<io::Error> for ZipError {
    fn from(err: io::Error) -> Self {
        ZipError::IoError(err.kind())
    }
}


// Type aliases to match C types
type mz_bool = bool;
type mz_uint = u32;
type mz_uint32 = u32;
type mz_uint64 = u64;
type mz_uint8 = u8;
type mz_uint16 = u16;
type mz_crc32 = u32;
type size_t = usize;

// CRC32 helpers
const MZ_CRC32_INIT: u32 = 0;


lazy_static::lazy_static! {
    static ref CRC32_TABLE: [u32; 256] = {
        let mut table = [0u32; 256];
        for i in 0..256 {
            let mut c = i as u32;
            for _ in 0..8 {
                if c & 1 != 0 {
                    c = 0xEDB88320 ^ (c >> 1);
                } else {
                    c >>= 1;
                }
            }
            table[i] = c;
        }
        table
    };
}

// Endian helpers
fn mz_read_le32(data: &[u8]) -> u32 {
    u32::from_le_bytes(data[..4].try_into().unwrap())
}

fn mz_read_le16(data: &[u8]) -> u16 {
    u16::from_le_bytes(data[..2].try_into().unwrap())
}

// File stat structure
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct mz_zip_archive_file_stat {
    pub m_is_directory: bool,
    pub m_is_supported: bool,
    pub m_method: u16,
    pub m_bit_flag: u16,
    pub m_local_header_ofs: u64,
    pub m_comp_size: u64,
    pub m_uncomp_size: u64,
    pub m_crc32: u32,
    pub m_time: u32,
}

// Callback types
pub type mz_file_write_func = fn(*mut c_void, mz_uint64, *const c_void, size_t) -> size_t;

// ZIP archive structure - simplified for extraction
pub struct mz_zip_archive {
    pub m_archive_size: u64,
    pub m_pState: Option<Box<mz_zip_internal_state>>,
    pub m_pRead: Option<Box<dyn Fn(*mut c_void, u64, *mut c_void, usize) -> usize>>,
    pub m_pAlloc: Option<Box<dyn Fn(*mut c_void, usize, usize) -> *mut c_void>>,
    pub m_pFree: Option<Box<dyn Fn(*mut c_void, *mut c_void)>>,
    pub m_pIO_opaque: *mut c_void,
    pub m_pAlloc_opaque: *mut c_void,
}

pub struct mz_zip_internal_state {
    pub m_pMem: Option<Vec<u8>>,
    // Other fields omitted for brevity
}

impl mz_zip_archive {
    fn set_error(&self, error: ZipError) -> mz_bool {
        // In real implementation, would set an error state
        false
    }
    
    fn read_data(&self, offset: u64, buf: &mut [u8]) -> ZipResult<usize> {
        if let Some(read_fn) = &self.m_pRead {
            let ptr = buf.as_mut_ptr() as *mut c_void;
            let result = read_fn(self.m_pIO_opaque, offset, ptr, buf.len());
            Ok(result)
        } else {
            Err(ZipError::InvalidParameter)
        }
    }
    
    fn allocate(&self, size: usize) -> ZipResult<Vec<u8>> {
        if let Some(alloc_fn) = &self.m_pAlloc {
            let ptr = alloc_fn(self.m_pAlloc_opaque, 1, size);
            if ptr.is_null() {
                return Err(ZipError::AllocFailed);
            }
            // In real code, we'd track this allocation
            let slice = unsafe { slice::from_raw_parts_mut(ptr as *mut u8, size) };
            let mut vec = vec![0u8; size];
            vec.copy_from_slice(slice);
            Ok(vec)
        } else {
            // Fallback to standard allocation
            Ok(vec![0u8; size])
        }
    }
    
    fn free_allocation(&self, _data: Vec<u8>) {
        // In real implementation, would call m_pFree
        // Data is dropped automatically
    }
}

// Inflator state (simplified)
#[derive(Default)]
struct tinfl_decompressor {
    // Internal state would go here
    _dummy: (),
}

impl tinfl_decompressor {
    fn init(&mut self) {
        // Initialize decompressor
    }
    
    fn decompress(
        &mut self,
        input: &[u8],
        output: &mut [u8],
        flags: u32,
    ) -> (usize, usize, i32) {
        // Simplified - in real code this would call the actual decompressor
        (input.len(), output.len(), 0 /* TINFL_STATUS_DONE */)
    }
}

// Status constants
const TINFL_STATUS_DONE: i32 = 0;
const TINFL_STATUS_FAILED: i32 = -1;
const TINFL_STATUS_NEEDS_MORE_INPUT: i32 = 1;
const TINFL_STATUS_HAS_MORE_OUTPUT: i32 = 2;
const TINFL_FLAG_HAS_MORE_INPUT: u32 = 8;

// Iterator state for streaming extraction
pub struct mz_zip_reader_extract_iter_state {
    pub pZip: *mut mz_zip_archive,
    pub flags: mz_uint,
    pub file_stat: mz_zip_archive_file_stat,
    pub status: i32,
    pub file_crc32: mz_crc32,
    pub read_buf_ofs: usize,
    pub out_buf_ofs: u64,
    pub pRead_buf: Option<Vec<u8>>,
    pub pWrite_buf: Option<Vec<u8>>,
    pub out_blk_remain: usize,
    pub cur_file_ofs: u64,
    pub read_buf_size: u64,
    pub read_buf_avail: u64,
    pub comp_remaining: u64,
    pub inflator: tinfl_decompressor,
}

// Main extraction function
pub fn mz_zip_reader_extract_to_callback(
    pZip: &mz_zip_archive,
    file_index: mz_uint,
    pCallback: mz_file_write_func,
    pOpaque: *mut c_void,
    flags: mz_uint,
) -> mz_bool {
    let mut file_crc32 = MZ_CRC32_INIT;
    
    // Validate parameters
    if pZip.m_pState.is_none() || pCallback as usize == 0 {
        return pZip.set_error(ZipError::InvalidParameter);
    }
    
    // Get file stat (simplified)
    let file_stat = match get_file_stat(pZip, file_index) {
        Ok(stat) => stat,
        Err(_) => return false,
    };
    
    // Directory or zero-length file
    if file_stat.m_is_directory || file_stat.m_comp_size == 0 {
        return true;
    }
    
    // Check for unsupported encryption
    if file_stat.m_bit_flag & (MZ_ZIP_GENERAL_PURPOSE_BIT_FLAG_IS_ENCRYPTED 
        | MZ_ZIP_GENERAL_PURPOSE_BIT_FLAG_USES_STRONG_ENCRYPTION 
        | MZ_ZIP_GENERAL_PURPOSE_BIT_FLAG_COMPRESSED_PATCH_FLAG) != 0 {
        return pZip.set_error(ZipError::UnsupportedEncryption);
    }
    
    // Check compression method
    if (flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_COMPRESSED_DATA) == 0 
        && file_stat.m_method != 0 
        && file_stat.m_method != MZ_DEFLATED {
        return pZip.set_error(ZipError::UnsupportedMethod);
    }
    
    // Read and validate local header
    let mut local_header = vec![0u8; MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE];
    if pZip.read_data(file_stat.m_local_header_ofs, &mut local_header)
        .map(|n| n != MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE)
        .unwrap_or(true) {
        return pZip.set_error(ZipError::FileReadFailed);
    }
    
    if mz_read_le32(&local_header) != MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIG {
        return pZip.set_error(ZipError::InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted);
    }
    
    let filename_len = mz_read_le16(&local_header[MZ_ZIP_LDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS..]) as u64;
    let extra_len = mz_read_le16(&local_header[MZ_ZIP_LDH_EXTRA_LEN_OFS..]) as u64;
    let mut cur_file_ofs = file_stat.m_local_header_ofs + MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as u64 + filename_len + extra_len;
    
    if cur_file_ofs + file_stat.m_comp_size > pZip.m_archive_size {
        return pZip.set_error(ZipError::InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted);
    }
    
    // Prepare buffers
    let (mut pRead_buf, read_buf_size, mut read_buf_avail, mut comp_remaining) = 
        if let Some(mem) = &pZip.m_pState.as_ref().unwrap().m_pMem {
            let offset = cur_file_ofs as usize;
            if offset + file_stat.m_comp_size as usize > mem.len() {
                return pZip.set_error(ZipError::InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted);
            }
            (Some(&mem[offset..]), file_stat.m_comp_size, file_stat.m_comp_size, 0)
        } else {
            let size = min(file_stat.m_comp_size, MZ_ZIP_MAX_IO_BUF_SIZE) as usize;
            match pZip.allocate(size) {
                Ok(buf) => (Some(buf), size as u64, 0, file_stat.m_comp_size),
                Err(_) => return pZip.set_error(ZipError::AllocFailed),
            }
        };
    
    let mut out_buf_ofs = 0u64;
    let mut status = TINFL_STATUS_DONE;
    let mut pWrite_buf: Option<Vec<u8>> = None;
    
    if (flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_COMPRESSED_DATA) != 0 || file_stat.m_method == 0 {
        // Stored data or compressed data requested
        if let Some(mem) = &pZip.m_pState.as_ref().unwrap().m_pMem {
            // Read from memory
            if let Some(read_slice) = pRead_buf {
                let write_result = unsafe {
                    pCallback(pOpaque, out_buf_ofs, read_slice.as_ptr() as *const c_void, read_slice.len())
                };
                if write_result != read_slice.len() {
                    pZip.set_error(ZipError::WriteCallbackFailed);
                    status = TINFL_STATUS_FAILED;
                } else if (flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_COMPRESSED_DATA) == 0 {
                    file_crc32 = mz_crc32(file_crc32, read_slice);
                }
            }
        } else {
            // Read from file in chunks
            while comp_remaining > 0 && status == TINFL_STATUS_DONE {
                read_buf_avail = min(read_buf_size, comp_remaining);
                if let Some(ref mut buf) = pRead_buf {
                    let read_size = pZip.read_data(cur_file_ofs, &mut buf[..read_buf_avail as usize])
                        .unwrap_or(0);
                    if read_size != read_buf_avail as usize {
                        pZip.set_error(ZipError::FileReadFailed);
                        status = TINFL_STATUS_FAILED;
                        break;
                    }
                    
                    if (flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_COMPRESSED_DATA) == 0 {
                        file_crc32 = mz_crc32(file_crc32, &buf[..read_buf_avail as usize]);
                    }
                    
                    let write_result = unsafe {
                        pCallback(pOpaque, out_buf_ofs, buf.as_ptr() as *const c_void, read_buf_avail as usize)
                    };
                    if write_result != read_buf_avail as usize {
                        pZip.set_error(ZipError::WriteCallbackFailed);
                        status = TINFL_STATUS_FAILED;
                        break;
                    }
                    
                    cur_file_ofs += read_buf_avail;
                    out_buf_ofs += read_buf_avail;
                    comp_remaining -= read_buf_avail;
                }
            }
        }
    } else {
        // Decompression required
        let mut inflator = tinfl_decompressor::default();
        inflator.init();
        
        pWrite_buf = match pZip.allocate(TINFL_LZ_DICT_SIZE) {
            Ok(buf) => Some(buf),
            Err(_) => {
                pZip.set_error(ZipError::AllocFailed);
                status = TINFL_STATUS_FAILED;
                None
            }
        };
        
        if let Some(ref mut write_buf) = pWrite_buf {
            while status == TINFL_STATUS_DONE || status == TINFL_STATUS_NEEDS_MORE_INPUT 
                || status == TINFL_STATUS_HAS_MORE_OUTPUT {
                
                let write_buf_cur_ofs = (out_buf_ofs as usize) & (TINFL_LZ_DICT_SIZE - 1);
                let out_buf_size = TINFL_LZ_DICT_SIZE - write_buf_cur_ofs;
                
                // Read more input if needed
                if read_buf_avail == 0 && pZip.m_pState.as_ref().unwrap().m_pMem.is_none() {
                    read_buf_avail = min(read_buf_size, comp_remaining);
                    if let Some(ref mut buf) = pRead_buf {
                        let read_size = pZip.read_data(cur_file_ofs, &mut buf[..read_buf_avail as usize])
                            .unwrap_or(0);
                        if read_size != read_buf_avail as usize {
                            pZip.set_error(ZipError::FileReadFailed);
                            status = TINFL_STATUS_FAILED;
                            break;
                        }
                        cur_file_ofs += read_buf_avail;
                        comp_remaining -= read_buf_avail;
                    }
                }
                
                // Decompress
                if let Some(ref read_buf) = pRead_buf {
                    let (in_consumed, out_produced, decomp_status) = inflator.decompress(
                        &read_buf[..read_buf_avail as usize],
                        &mut write_buf[write_buf_cur_ofs..write_buf_cur_ofs + out_buf_size],
                        if comp_remaining > 0 { TINFL_FLAG_HAS_MORE_INPUT } else { 0 },
                    );
                    
                    read_buf_avail -= in_consumed as u64;
                    status = decomp_status;
                    
                    if out_produced > 0 {
                        let write_result = unsafe {
                            pCallback(
                                pOpaque,
                                out_buf_ofs,
                                &write_buf[write_buf_cur_ofs] as *const u8 as *const c_void,
                                out_produced,
                            )
                        };
                        if write_result != out_produced {
                            pZip.set_error(ZipError::WriteCallbackFailed);
                            status = TINFL_STATUS_FAILED;
                            break;
                        }
                        
                        file_crc32 = mz_crc32(file_crc32, &write_buf[write_buf_cur_ofs..write_buf_cur_ofs + out_produced]);
                        
                        out_buf_ofs += out_produced as u64;
                        if out_buf_ofs > file_stat.m_uncomp_size {
                            pZip.set_error(ZipError::DecompressionFailed);
                            status = TINFL_STATUS_FAILED;
                            break;
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    
    // Final validation
    if status == TINFL_STATUS_DONE && (flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_COMPRESSED_DATA) == 0 {
        if out_buf_ofs != file_stat.m_uncomp_size {
            pZip.set_error(ZipError::UnexpectedDecompressedSize);
            status = TINFL_STATUS_FAILED;
        } else if file_crc32 != file_stat.m_crc32 {
            pZip.set_error(ZipError::DecompressionFailed);
            status = TINFL_STATUS_FAILED;
        }
    }
    
    // Cleanup
    if let Some(buf) = pRead_buf {
        pZip.free_allocation(buf);
    }
    if let Some(buf) = pWrite_buf {
        pZip.free_allocation(buf);
    }
    
    status == TINFL_STATUS_DONE
}

// Helper function (simplified)
fn get_file_stat(pZip: &mz_zip_archive, file_index: mz_uint) -> ZipResult<mz_zip_archive_file_stat> {
    // Simplified - real implementation would read from central directory
    Ok(mz_zip_archive_file_stat {
        m_is_directory: false,
        m_is_supported: true,
        m_method: 0,
        m_bit_flag: 0,
        m_local_header_ofs: 0,
        m_comp_size: 0,
        m_uncomp_size: 0,
        m_crc32: 0,
        m_time: 0,
    })
}

pub fn mz_zip_reader_extract_file_to_callback(
    pZip: &mz_zip_archive,
    pFilename: &str,
    pCallback: mz_file_write_func,
    pOpaque: *mut c_void,
    flags: mz_uint,
) -> mz_bool {
    // Simplified file lookup
    let file_index = match locate_file(pZip, pFilename, flags) {
        Some(idx) => idx,
        None => return false,
    };
    
    mz_zip_reader_extract_to_callback(pZip, file_index, pCallback, pOpaque, flags)
}

// Simplified file location
fn locate_file(pZip: &mz_zip_archive, filename: &str, flags: mz_uint) -> Option<mz_uint> {
    // In real implementation, would search central directory
    Some(0)
}

// File extraction helper
fn mz_zip_file_write_callback(pOpaque: *mut c_void, ofs: mz_uint64, pBuf: *const c_void, n: size_t) -> size_t {
    
    // This is platform-specific in C, simplified for Rust
    unsafe {
        let file = &mut *(pOpaque as *mut File);
        let slice = slice::from_raw_parts(pBuf as *const u8, n);
        if let Ok(_) = file.write_all(slice) {
            n
        } else {
            0
        }
    }
}

pub fn mz_zip_reader_extract_to_file(
    pZip: &mz_zip_archive,
    file_index: mz_uint,
    pDst_filename: &str,
    flags: mz_uint,
) -> mz_bool {
    // Get file stat
    let file_stat = match get_file_stat(pZip, file_index) {
        Ok(stat) => stat,
        Err(_) => return false,
    };
    
    if file_stat.m_is_directory || !file_stat.m_is_supported {
        return pZip.set_error(ZipError::UnsupportedFeature);
    }
    
    // Open output file
    let mut file = match File::create(pDst_filename) {
        Ok(f) => f,
        Err(_) => return pZip.set_error(ZipError::FileOpenFailed),
    };
    
    // Extract using callback
    let status = mz_zip_reader_extract_to_callback(
        pZip,
        file_index,
        mz_zip_file_write_callback,
        &mut file as *mut _ as *mut c_void,
        flags,
    );
    
    // Close file and check for errors
    if let Err(e) = file.sync_all() {
        if status {
            pZip.set_error(ZipError::FileCloseFailed);
        }
        return false;
    }
    
    status
}

pub fn mz_zip_reader_extract_file_to_file(
    pZip: &mz_zip_archive,
    pArchive_filename: &str,
    pDst_filename: &str,
    flags: mz_uint,
) -> mz_bool {
    let file_index = match locate_file(pZip, pArchive_filename, flags) {
        Some(idx) => idx,
        None => return false,
    };
    
    mz_zip_reader_extract_to_file(pZip, file_index, pDst_filename, flags)
}

// CRC32 computation callback
fn mz_zip_compute_crc32_callback(pOpaque: *mut c_void, file_ofs: mz_uint64, pBuf: *const c_void, n: size_t) -> size_t {
    unsafe {
        let crc_ptr = pOpaque as *mut mz_crc32;
        let slice = slice::from_raw_parts(pBuf as *const u8, n);
        *crc_ptr = mz_crc32(*crc_ptr, slice);
    }
    n
}

// --- Module: mz_p5 ---
//! ZIP archive validation and writing functions


// Constants from miniz.h
const MZ_ZIP_LDH_COMPRESSED_SIZE_OFS: usize = 18;
const MZ_ZIP_LDH_DECOMPRESSED_SIZE_OFS: usize = 22;
const MZ_ZIP_LDH_CRC32_OFS: usize = 14;
const MZ_ZIP_LDH_BIT_FLAG_OFS: usize = 6;
const MZ_ZIP_DATA_DESCRIPTOR_ID: u32 = 0x08074b50;

// Error type
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]

impl std::fmt::Display for ZipError {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        write!(f, "{:?}", self)
    }
}

impl std::error::Error for ZipError {}

// Core types
pub type MzBool = bool;
pub type MzUint = u32;
pub type MzUint32 = u32;
pub type MzUint64 = u64;
pub type MzUint16 = u16;
pub type MzUint8 = u8;

const MZ_TRUE: MzBool = true;
const MZ_FALSE: MzBool = false;

// Dynamic array similar to mz_zip_array

impl<T> ZipArray<T> {
    pub fn new(element_size: usize) -> Self {
        Self {
            data: Vec::new(),
            element_size,
        }
    }

    pub fn init(&mut self, element_size: usize) {
        self.element_size = element_size;
        self.data.clear();
    }

    pub fn resize(&mut self, new_size: usize, zero_new: bool) -> Result<(), ZipError> {
        self.data.resize_with(new_size, || {
            if zero_new {
                unsafe { std::mem::zeroed() }
            } else {
                std::mem::MaybeUninit::zeroed().assume_init()
            }
        });
        Ok(())
    }

    pub fn clear(&mut self) {
        self.data.clear();
    }

    pub fn as_ptr(&self) -> *const T {
        self.data.as_ptr()
    }

    pub fn as_mut_ptr(&mut self) -> *mut T {
        self.data.as_mut_ptr()
    }

    pub fn len(&self) -> usize {
        self.data.len()
    }

    pub fn is_empty(&self) -> bool {
        self.data.is_empty()
    }
}

impl ZipArray<u8> {
    pub fn as_bytes(&self) -> &[u8] {
        &self.data
    }

    pub fn as_bytes_mut(&mut self) -> &mut [u8] {
        &mut self.data
    }
}

// File stat structure
pub struct ZipArchiveFileStat {
    pub m_filename: String,
    pub m_uncomp_size: u64,
    pub m_comp_size: u64,
    pub m_crc32: u32,
    pub m_local_header_ofs: u64,
    pub m_is_directory: bool,
    pub m_is_encrypted: bool,
    pub m_method: u16,
    pub m_is_supported: bool,
}

// Internal state

// Zip archive (simplified for this module)

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]

// Callback types
pub type ReadCallback = Box<dyn Fn(&mut dyn std::any::Any, u64, &mut [u8]) -> Result<usize, ZipError>>;
pub type WriteCallback = Box<dyn Fn(&mut dyn std::any::Any, u64, &[u8]) -> Result<usize, ZipError>>;
pub type AllocCallback = Box<dyn Fn(&mut dyn std::any::Any, usize, usize) -> *mut std::ffi::c_void>;
pub type FreeCallback = Box<dyn Fn(&mut dyn std::any::Any, *mut std::ffi::c_void)>;
pub type ReallocCallback = Box<dyn Fn(&mut dyn std::any::Any, *mut std::ffi::c_void, usize, usize) -> *mut std::ffi::c_void>;

impl ZipArchive {
    pub fn zero_struct() -> Self {
        Self {
            m_pstate: None,
            m_total_files: 0,
            m_archive_size: 0,
            m_last_error: ZipError::NoError,
            m_zip_type: ZipType::User,
            m_zip_mode: ZipMode::Invalid,
            m_file_offset_alignment: 0,
            m_central_directory_file_ofs: 0,
        }
    }

    fn set_error(&mut self, error: ZipError) -> MzBool {
        self.m_last_error = error;
        MZ_FALSE
    }
}

// Helper functions
fn read_le_u16(bytes: &[u8]) -> u16 {
    u16::from_le_bytes([bytes[0], bytes[1]])
}

fn read_le_u32(bytes: &[u8]) -> u32 {
    u32::from_le_bytes([bytes[0], bytes[1], bytes[2], bytes[3]])
}

fn read_le_u64(bytes: &[u8]) -> u64 {
    u64::from_le_bytes([
        bytes[0], bytes[1], bytes[2], bytes[3],
        bytes[4], bytes[5], bytes[6], bytes[7],
    ])
}

// Validation functions
pub fn mz_zip_validate_file(
    pzip: &mut ZipArchive,
    file_index: u32,
    flags: u32,
) -> Result<bool, ZipError> {
    // Check parameters
    if pzip.m_pstate.is_none() {
        pzip.set_error(ZipError::InvalidParameter);
        return Ok(false);
    }

    if file_index >= pzip.m_total_files {
        pzip.set_error(ZipError::InvalidParameter);
        return Ok(false);
    }

    let pstate = pzip.m_pstate.as_ref().unwrap();

    // Get central directory header (simplified)
    let p_central_dir_header = {
        let central_dir = &pstate.m_central_dir;
        if central_dir.is_empty() {
            pzip.set_error(ZipError::InvalidParameter);
            return Ok(false);
        }
        // Simplified - assume we can access it
        central_dir.as_bytes()
    };

    // Get file stat (simplified)
    let file_stat = match mz_zip_file_stat_internal(pzip, file_index, p_central_dir_header) {
        Ok(stat) => stat,
        Err(e) => {
            pzip.set_error(e);
            return Ok(false);
        }
    };

    // A directory or zero length file
    if file_stat.m_is_directory || file_stat.m_uncomp_size == 0 {
        return Ok(true);
    }

    // Encryption and patch files are not supported
    if file_stat.m_is_encrypted {
        pzip.set_error(ZipError::UnsupportedEncryption);
        return Ok(false);
    }

    // Only support stored and deflate
    if file_stat.m_method != 0 && file_stat.m_method != 8 {
        pzip.set_error(ZipError::UnsupportedMethod);
        return Ok(false);
    }

    if !file_stat.m_is_supported {
        pzip.set_error(ZipError::UnsupportedFeature);
        return Ok(false);
    }

    // Read and parse local directory entry
    let mut local_header_buf = [0u8; MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE];
    let local_header_ofs = file_stat.m_local_header_ofs;
    
    // Simplified read - assume we have a file
    if let Some(file) = &mut pstate.m_pfile {
        file.seek(SeekFrom::Start(local_header_ofs)).map_err(|_| {
            pzip.set_error(ZipError::FileReadFailed);
            ZipError::FileReadFailed
        })?;
        
        file.read_exact(&mut local_header_buf).map_err(|_| {
            pzip.set_error(ZipError::FileReadFailed);
            ZipError::FileReadFailed
        })?;
    } else {
        // Memory archive
        if let Some(mem) = &pstate.m_pmem {
            let start = local_header_ofs as usize;
            let end = start + MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE;
            if end > mem.len() {
                pzip.set_error(ZipError::InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted);
                return Ok(false);
            }
            local_header_buf.copy_from_slice(&mem[start..end]);
        } else {
            pzip.set_error(ZipError::FileReadFailed);
            return Ok(false);
        }
    }

    if read_le_u32(&local_header_buf) != MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIG {
        pzip.set_error(ZipError::InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted);
        return Ok(false);
    }

    let local_header_filename_len = read_le_u16(&local_header_buf[MZ_ZIP_LDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS..]) as usize;
    let local_header_extra_len = read_le_u16(&local_header_buf[MZ_ZIP_LDH_EXTRA_LEN_OFS..]) as usize;
    let mut local_header_comp_size = read_le_u32(&local_header_buf[MZ_ZIP_LDH_COMPRESSED_SIZE_OFS..]) as u64;
    let mut local_header_uncomp_size = read_le_u32(&local_header_buf[MZ_ZIP_LDH_DECOMPRESSED_SIZE_OFS..]) as u64;
    let local_header_crc32 = read_le_u32(&local_header_buf[MZ_ZIP_LDH_CRC32_OFS..]);
    let local_header_bit_flags = read_le_u16(&local_header_buf[MZ_ZIP_LDH_BIT_FLAG_OFS..]);
    let has_data_descriptor = (local_header_bit_flags & 8) != 0;

    if local_header_filename_len != file_stat.m_filename.len() {
        pzip.set_error(ZipError::InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted);
        return Ok(false);
    }

    if (local_header_ofs + MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as u64 + local_header_filename_len as u64 + 
        local_header_extra_len as u64 + file_stat.m_comp_size) > pzip.m_archive_size {
        pzip.set_error(ZipError::InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted);
        return Ok(false);
    }

    // Validate filename
    if local_header_filename_len > 0 {
        let mut filename_buf = vec![0u8; local_header_filename_len];
        
        if let Some(file) = &mut pstate.m_pfile {
            file.seek(SeekFrom::Start(local_header_ofs + MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as u64))
                .map_err(|_| ZipError::FileReadFailed)?;
            file.read_exact(&mut filename_buf).map_err(|_| ZipError::FileReadFailed)?;
        } else if let Some(mem) = &pstate.m_pmem {
            let start = (local_header_ofs + MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as u64) as usize;
            let end = start + local_header_filename_len;
            if end > mem.len() {
                pzip.set_error(ZipError::FileReadFailed);
                return Ok(false);
            }
            filename_buf.copy_from_slice(&mem[start..end]);
        }
        
        if filename_buf != file_stat.m_filename.as_bytes() {
            pzip.set_error(ZipError::ValidationFailed);
            return Ok(false);
        }
    }

    // Check for ZIP64 extended info in local header
    let mut found_zip64_ext_data_in_ldir = false;
    if local_header_extra_len > 0 && (local_header_comp_size == MZ_UINT32_MAX || local_header_uncomp_size == MZ_UINT32_MAX) {
        let mut extra_buf = vec![0u8; local_header_extra_len];
        
        if let Some(file) = &mut pstate.m_pfile {
            file.seek(SeekFrom::Start(local_header_ofs + MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as u64 + local_header_filename_len as u64))
                .map_err(|_| ZipError::FileReadFailed)?;
            file.read_exact(&mut extra_buf).map_err(|_| ZipError::FileReadFailed)?;
        } else if let Some(mem) = &pstate.m_pmem {
            let start = (local_header_ofs + MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as u64 + local_header_filename_len as u64) as usize;
            let end = start + local_header_extra_len;
            if end > mem.len() {
                pzip.set_error(ZipError::FileReadFailed);
                return Ok(false);
            }
            extra_buf.copy_from_slice(&mem[start..end]);
        }
        
        let mut offset = 0;
        while offset + 4 <= local_header_extra_len {
            let field_id = read_le_u16(&extra_buf[offset..]);
            let field_data_size = read_le_u16(&extra_buf[offset + 2..]) as usize;
            let field_total_size = field_data_size + 4;
            
            if offset + field_total_size > local_header_extra_len {
                pzip.set_error(ZipError::InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted);
                return Ok(false);
            }
            
            if field_id == MZ_ZIP64_EXTENDED_INFORMATION_FIELD_HEADER_ID {
                if field_data_size >= 16 {
                    local_header_uncomp_size = read_le_u64(&extra_buf[offset + 4..]);
                    local_header_comp_size = read_le_u64(&extra_buf[offset + 12..]);
                    found_zip64_ext_data_in_ldir = true;
                }
                break;
            }
            
            offset += field_total_size;
        }
    }

    // Check data descriptor if present
    if has_data_descriptor && local_header_comp_size == 0 && local_header_crc32 == 0 {
        let num_descriptor_uint32s = if pstate.m_zip64 || found_zip64_ext_data_in_ldir { 6 } else { 4 };
        let descriptor_size = num_descriptor_uint32s * 4;
        let mut descriptor_buf = vec![0u8; descriptor_size];
        
        let descriptor_offset = local_header_ofs + MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as u64 + 
            local_header_filename_len as u64 + local_header_extra_len as u64 + file_stat.m_comp_size;
        
        if let Some(file) = &mut pstate.m_pfile {
            file.seek(SeekFrom::Start(descriptor_offset)).map_err(|_| ZipError::FileReadFailed)?;
            file.read_exact(&mut descriptor_buf).map_err(|_| ZipError::FileReadFailed)?;
        } else if let Some(mem) = &pstate.m_pmem {
            let start = descriptor_offset as usize;
            let end = start + descriptor_size;
            if end > mem.len() {
                pzip.set_error(ZipError::FileReadFailed);
                return Ok(false);
            }
            descriptor_buf.copy_from_slice(&mem[start..end]);
        }
        
        let has_id = read_le_u32(&descriptor_buf) == MZ_ZIP_DATA_DESCRIPTOR_ID;
        let p_src = if has_id { 4 } else { 0 };
        
        let file_crc32 = read_le_u32(&descriptor_buf[p_src..]);
        let comp_size;
        let uncomp_size;
        
        if pstate.m_zip64 || found_zip64_ext_data_in_ldir {
            comp_size = read_le_u64(&descriptor_buf[p_src + 4..]);
            uncomp_size = read_le_u64(&descriptor_buf[p_src + 12..]);
        } else {
            comp_size = read_le_u32(&descriptor_buf[p_src + 4..]) as u64;
            uncomp_size = read_le_u32(&descriptor_buf[p_src + 8..]) as u64;
        }
        
        if file_crc32 != file_stat.m_crc32 || comp_size != file_stat.m_comp_size || 
           uncomp_size != file_stat.m_uncomp_size {
            pzip.set_error(ZipError::ValidationFailed);
            return Ok(false);
        }
    } else {
        if local_header_crc32 != file_stat.m_crc32 || local_header_comp_size != file_stat.m_comp_size || 
           local_header_uncomp_size != file_stat.m_uncomp_size {
            pzip.set_error(ZipError::ValidationFailed);
            return Ok(false);
        }
    }

    // Validate file contents if requested
    if flags & 1 == 0 {  // MZ_ZIP_FLAG_VALIDATE_HEADERS_ONLY
        // Simplified - would need actual decompression here
        // For now, just return success
    }

    Ok(true)
}

fn mz_zip_file_stat_internal(
    _pzip: &ZipArchive,
    _file_index: u32,
    _central_dir_header: &[u8],
) -> Result<ZipArchiveFileStat, ZipError> {
    // Simplified implementation
    // In real code, this would parse the central directory header
    Ok(ZipArchiveFileStat {
        m_filename: String::new(),
        m_uncomp_size: 0,
        m_comp_size: 0,
        m_crc32: 0,
        m_local_header_ofs: 0,
        m_is_directory: false,
        m_is_encrypted: false,
        m_method: 0,
        m_is_supported: true,
    })
}

pub fn mz_zip_validate_archive(pzip: &mut ZipArchive, flags: u32) -> Result<bool, ZipError> {
    if pzip.m_pstate.is_none() {
        pzip.set_error(ZipError::InvalidParameter);
        return Ok(false);
    }

    let pstate = pzip.m_pstate.as_ref().unwrap();

    // Basic sanity checks
    if !pstate.m_zip64 {
        if pzip.m_total_files as usize > MZ_UINT16_MAX {
            pzip.set_error(ZipError::ArchiveTooLarge);
            return Ok(false);
        }

        if pzip.m_archive_size > MZ_UINT32_MAX {
            pzip.set_error(ZipError::ArchiveTooLarge);
            return Ok(false);
        }
    } else {
        if pstate.m_central_dir.len() as u64 >= MZ_UINT32_MAX {
            pzip.set_error(ZipError::ArchiveTooLarge);
            return Ok(false);
        }
    }

    for i in 0..pzip.m_total_files {
        if flags & 2 != 0 {  // MZ_ZIP_FLAG_VALIDATE_LOCATE_FILE_FLAG
            // Simplified locate file check
            // In real code, would call mz_zip_reader_locate_file_v2
        }

        if !mz_zip_validate_file(pzip, i, flags)? {
            return Ok(false);
        }
    }

    Ok(true)
}

pub fn mz_zip_validate_mem_archive(
    pmem: &[u8],
    _size: usize,
    flags: u32,
    perr: &mut ZipError,
) -> Result<bool, ZipError> {
    if pmem.is_empty() {
        *perr = ZipError::InvalidParameter;
        return Ok(false);
    }

    let mut zip = ZipArchive::zero_struct();
    
    // Simplified init - in real code would call mz_zip_reader_init_mem
    zip.m_pstate = Some(Box::new(ZipInternalState {
        m_central_dir: ZipArray::new(1),
        m_central_dir_offsets: ZipArray::new(4),
        m_sorted_central_dir_offsets: ZipArray::new(4),
        m_init_flags: 0,
        m_zip64: false,
        m_zip64_has_extended_info_fields: false,
        m_pfile: None,
        m_file_archive_start_ofs: 0,
        m_pmem: Some(pmem.to_vec()),
        m_mem_size: pmem.len(),
        m_mem_capacity: pmem.len(),
    }));

    let mut actual_err = ZipError::NoError;
    let mut success = true;

    if !mz_zip_validate_archive(&mut zip, flags)? {
        actual_err = zip.m_last_error;
        success = false;
    }

    // Simplified end - in real code would call mz_zip_reader_end_internal
    zip.m_pstate = None;

    *perr = actual_err;
    Ok(success)
}

pub fn mz_zip_validate_file_archive(
    filename: &str,
    flags: u32,
    perr: &mut ZipError,
) -> Result<bool, ZipError> {
    if filename.is_empty() {
        *perr = ZipError::InvalidParameter;
        return Ok(false);
    }

    let mut zip = ZipArchive::zero_struct();
    
    // Try to open the file
    let file = File::open(filename).map_err(|_| {
        *perr = ZipError::FileOpenFailed;
        ZipError::FileOpenFailed
    })?;

    zip.m_pstate = Some(Box::new(ZipInternalState {
        m_central_dir: ZipArray::new(1),
        m_central_dir_offsets: ZipArray::new(4),
        m_sorted_central_dir_offsets: ZipArray::new(4),
        m_init_flags: 0,
        m_zip64: false,
        m_zip64_has_extended_info_fields: false,
        m_pfile: Some(file),
        m_file_archive_start_ofs: 0,
        m_pmem: None,
        m_mem_size: 0,
        m_mem_capacity: 0,
    }));

    let mut actual_err = ZipError::NoError;
    let mut success = true;

    if !mz_zip_validate_archive(&mut zip, flags)? {
        actual_err = zip.m_last_error;
        success = false;
    }

    // Close file
    zip.m_pstate = None;

    *perr = actual_err;
    Ok(success)
}

// Little-endian write functions
pub fn mz_write_le16(p: &mut [u8], v: u16) {
    p[0] = v as u8;
    p[1] = (v >> 8) as u8;
}

pub fn mz_write_le32(p: &mut [u8], v: u32) {
    p[0] = v as u8;
    p[1] = (v >> 8) as u8;
    p[2] = (v >> 16) as u8;
    p[3] = (v >> 24) as u8;
}

pub fn mz_write_le64(p: &mut [u8], v: u64) {
    mz_write_le32(p, v as u32);
    mz_write_le32(&mut p[4..], (v >> 32) as u32);
}

// Heap write function
pub fn mz_zip_heap_write_func(
    popaque: &mut dyn std::any::Any,
    file_ofs: u64,
    pbuf: &[u8],
    n: usize,
) -> Result<usize, ZipError> {
    let pzip = popaque.downcast_mut::<ZipArchive>().ok_or(ZipError::InvalidParameter)?;
    let pstate = pzip.m_pstate.as_mut().ok_or(ZipError::InvalidParameter)?;

    if n == 0 {
        return Ok(0);
    }

    let new_size = std::cmp::max(file_ofs + n as u64, pstate.m_mem_size as u64);

    // Check for 32-bit overflow
    if usize::BITS == 32 && new_size > 0x7FFFFFFF {
        pzip.set_error(ZipError::FileTooLarge);
        return Ok(0);
    }

    if new_size as usize > pstate.m_mem_capacity {
        let mut new_capacity = std::cmp::max(64, pstate.m_mem_capacity);
        while new_capacity < new_size as usize {
            new_capacity = new_capacity.saturating_mul(2);
        }

        if let Some(mem) = &mut pstate.m_pmem {
            mem.resize(new_capacity, 0);
            pstate.m_mem_capacity = new_capacity;
        } else {
            pstate.m_pmem = Some(vec![0u8; new_capacity]);
            pstate.m_mem_capacity = new_capacity;
        }
    }

    if let Some(mem) = &mut pstate.m_pmem {
        let start = file_ofs as usize;
        let end = start + n;
        if end <= mem.len() {
            mem[start..end].copy_from_slice(&pbuf[..n]);
            pstate.m_mem_size = new_size as usize;
            Ok(n)
        } else {
            pzip.set_error(ZipError::AllocFailed);
            Ok(0)
        }
    } else {
        pzip.set_error(ZipError::AllocFailed);
        Ok(0)
    }
}

// Writer end internal
pub fn mz_zip_writer_end_internal(pzip: &mut ZipArchive, set_last_error: bool) -> Result<bool, ZipError> {
    if pzip.m_pstate.is_none() || pzip.m_zip_mode == ZipMode::Invalid {
        if set_last_error {
            pzip.set_error(ZipError::InvalidParameter);
        }
        return Ok(false);
    }

    let mut pstate = pzip.m_pstate.take().unwrap();

    // Clear arrays
    pstate.m_central_dir.clear();
    pstate.m_central_dir_offsets.clear();
    pstate.m_sorted_central_dir_offsets.clear();

    // Close file if open
    if let Some(file) = pstate.m_pfile.take() {
        if pzip.m_zip_type == ZipType::File {
            drop(file); // File closes automatically when dropped
        }
    }

    // Free memory if using heap write
    // Note: In Rust, memory is automatically freed when pstate is dropped
    pstate.m_pmem = None;

    pzip.m_zip_mode = ZipMode::Invalid;
    Ok(true)
}

// Writer initialization
pub fn mz_zip_writer_init_v2(pzip: &mut ZipArchive, existing_size: u64, flags: u32) -> Result<bool, ZipError> {
    let zip64 = (flags & 0x1000) != 0;  // MZ_ZIP_FLAG_WRITE_ZIP64

    if pzip.m_pstate.is_some() || pzip.m_zip_mode != ZipMode::Invalid {
        pzip.set_error(ZipError::InvalidParameter);
        return Ok(false);
    }

    if flags & 0x2000 != 0 {  // MZ_ZIP_FLAG_WRITE_ALLOW_READING
        // Would need read callback
    }
